Generated by `run_study.py` from `results.json`; do not edit by hand. The frozen specification is in `README.md` and `candidate_seed.json` (file sha256 `5a79797ee919ed99e55a1f890f995510374ff4d21c16a13ba230e5f7faf3633b`, protocol sha256 `3ebd8bf2a80638e27812b63c2b6df0cdcffed38fccd3a305b040295f784f4634`).

Funding dependency #1731 and the On Balance Volume evaluator from #1657 are present at the baseline. This study reuses the manifest funding attachment and adds no funding option or funding accounting. No Binance US proxy leg and no cache-based `--funding` run is part of this study.

## Protocol

- Close owner `tiered_tp_pct` with params `{"tp_tiers": [{"close_fraction": 1.0, "profit_pct": 0.02}]}`, comparison mode `strict`, intrabar resolution `ohlc_walk`, stop owner fixed entry ATR x1.0, capital $1000 per dataset; signals on bar N fill at the bar N+1 open.
- Arms: `klinger_long` (`klinger_divergence`, long, grid), `klinger_both` (`klinger_divergence`, both, grid), `ablation_long` (`klinger_divergence`, long, inherit), `ablation_both` (`klinger_divergence`, both, inherit), `obv_long` (`on_balance_volume_divergence`, long, grid), `obv_both` (`on_balance_volume_divergence`, both, grid), `volume_weighted` (`volume_weighted`, long, grid).
- Comparisons: `klinger_long` against `volume_weighted`, `obv_long`, `ablation_long`; `klinger_both` against `obv_both`, `ablation_both`.
- Selection on `train` only: complete candle and funding coverage, every dataset scored, no liquidated leg, no incomplete-funding leg, at least min_train_independent_positions independent position clusters; clusters: position intervals [entry, exit] pooled across all datasets and merged when they overlap; each merged interval is one cluster; order max mean net Sharpe; max mean drawdown-adjusted return; earliest grid order. Each ablation inherits its candidate arm's selected parameters and changes only `divergence_test` to false.
- Held-out `test`: at least 30 independent position clusters for each primary candidate arm; the bidirectional arm needs long and short positions.
- Cost runs: base x1 (manifest fees, per-coin half spread and slippage); stress x2 (manifest fees unchanged, half spread and slippage doubled (the manifest cost multiplier does not scale fees)).
- Pass rule: complete coverage and samples, both primary candidate arms net PnL > 0 at base and stress cost, and at base cost each primary arm has a strictly higher mean net return than each of its comparators and a worst drawdown no larger than each of its comparators.
- `volume_weighted` registers long-only entries, so its comparison uses the matched long-only arm. A comparator long-exit signal is never treated as a short entry.

## Finite replay versus continuous history

Parameters `{"divergence_test": true, "fast_period": 34, "left_span": 2, "max_separation": 40, "min_separation": 5, "replay_bars": 112, "right_span": 2, "setup_expiry": 6, "signal_period": 13, "slow_period": 55}` (the selected `klinger_both` parameters). The continuous reference runs the same recurrence once over the whole manifest frame from its first row and applies the same pivot, pairing, divergence, cross, expiry and consumption rules. The finite evaluator restarts trend, cm, both EMAs and the signal line `replay_bars` back for every endpoint.

- Scored rows 13140; finite valid rows 13140; finite zero_cm holds 0.
- Entries: finite 343, continuous 344, both on the same bar 323, same side 323.
- Decision mismatches 41 (share 0.0031): finite_invalid_hold 0, continuous_invalid 0, seed_divergence 41, unclassified 0.
- The cm accumulation restarts at the replay seed, and the volume-force factor depends on dm / cm, so the finite oscillator differs from the continuous one even far from the seed; no equivalence with any platform's built-in Klinger implementation is claimed.

| Dataset | Window | Scored | Finite valid | Finite entries | Continuous entries | Same side | Mismatches | Seed divergence | Unclassified | Median abs osc diff | p95 rel osc diff | Sign agreement |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| BTC 4h | train | 2190 | 2190 | 61 | 62 | 58 | 7 | 7 | 0 | 701.042 | 0.5333 | 0.9872 |
| BTC 4h | test | 2190 | 2190 | 51 | 51 | 47 | 8 | 8 | 0 | 1571.587 | 0.7038 | 0.9845 |
| ETH 4h | train | 2190 | 2190 | 54 | 57 | 51 | 9 | 9 | 0 | 17177.361 | 0.5311 | 0.9849 |
| ETH 4h | test | 2190 | 2190 | 53 | 53 | 50 | 6 | 6 | 0 | 22147.156 | 0.6416 | 0.9854 |

## Close validation

- Modes `strict`, eligibility `eligible`, parity status `unverified`, incomplete parity no.
- `unverified` is the strict, eligible capability status (no approximation and no refusal). It is not a ledger-verified claim that these exits equal live exits.

## Parity

`backtest/candidates/klinger_divergence_1662/parity_results.json` sha256 `b384dfee8847eae8`, clean yes (decision yes, indicator yes).
- Decision parity: the last 600 held-out bars of every dataset, the selected `klinger_both` parameters and the frozen close owner, direction both, with a 200-row live window and with every prefix; the composer mode compares full-frame decisions with the per-bar live composer and the batched paper mode also compares solo and batched explicit-paper `check_hyperliquid.py` slots.
- Indicator parity: for every scored row of both windows, the full-frame row equals the row computed from its own prefix, its trailing 200 rows and its exact support span (oscillator values within 1e-09 relative tolerance; signal, reason, validity, setup states and pivot timestamps exactly).
- Admission and timed closed-bar behavior are proved by `shared_scripts/test_klinger_divergence_checks.py`.
